── FILE: scripts/init-db.mjs  (87 خط) ──

/**
 * اجرای مهاجرت‌های دیتابیس (بدون نیاز به سرور PostgreSQL).
 * همهٔ فایل‌های drizzle/*.sql به ترتیب اجرا می‌شوند و نامشان در جدول
 * `_migrations` ثبت می‌شود؛ بنابراین اجرای دوباره بی‌خطر است.
 *
 *   node scripts/init-db.mjs
 */
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { existsSync, readFileSync, unlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { mkdir, readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { requireLocalSqlite } from "../src/lib/sqlite-cloud.ts";

const dataDir = process.env.PGLITE_DATA_DIR ?? ".data/pglite";
try {
  requireLocalSqlite(dataDir);
} catch (error) {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
  process.exit(1);
}
await mkdir(dataDir, { recursive: true });

/**
 * قفل: اگر سامانه همین حالا روشن است، مهاجرت اجرا نشود تا فایل‌های دیتابیس خراب نشوند.
 */
const lockFile = path.join(path.resolve(dataDir), ".pumpstation.lock");
if (existsSync(lockFile)) {
  try {
    const info = JSON.parse(readFileSync(lockFile, "utf8"));
    if (info?.pid) {
      try {
        process.kill(info.pid, 0);
        console.error(`[migrate] سامانه در حال اجراست (PID ${info.pid}). ابتدا آن را ببندید، بعد مهاجرت را اجرا کنید.`);
        process.exit(1);
      } catch {
        console.warn(`[migrate] قفل قدیمی (PID ${info.pid}) پیدا شد؛ ادامه می‌دهیم.`);
      }
    }
  } catch {
    /* قفل ناقص؛ ادامه */
  }
}
writeFileSync(lockFile, JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, startedAt: Date.now(), dir: path.resolve(dataDir) }), "utf8");
const client = new PGlite(dataDir);

await client.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "_migrations" ("name" text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, "applied_at" text NOT NULL);`);

const applied = new Set((await client.query(`SELECT name FROM "_migrations"`)).rows.map((r) => r.name));

// دیتابیسی که قبل از اضافه‌شدن جدول مهاجرت ساخته شده: مهاجرت اول را ثبت‌شده فرض کن
if (applied.size === 0) {
  const legacy = await client.query(`SELECT to_regclass('public.settings') AS tbl`);
  if (legacy.rows?.[0]?.tbl) {
    await client.query(`INSERT INTO "_migrations" ("name","applied_at") VALUES ($1,$2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, [
      "0000_baseline",
      new Date().toISOString(),
    ]);
    applied.add("0000_baseline");
  }
}

const files = (await readdir("drizzle")).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".sql")).sort();
for (const file of files) {
  const name = file.replace(/\.sql$/, "");
  const baseline = name.startsWith("0000_") && applied.has("0000_baseline");
  if (applied.has(name) || baseline) {
    console.log(`[migrate] skip ${file}`);
    continue;
  }
  const raw = await readFile(path.join("drizzle", file), "utf8");
  const statements = raw
    .split("--> statement-breakpoint")
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  for (const statement of statements) await client.exec(statement);
  await client.query(`INSERT INTO "_migrations" ("name","applied_at") VALUES ($1,$2)`, [name, new Date().toISOString()]);
  console.log(`[migrate] applied ${file} (${statements.length} statements)`);
}

await client.close();
try {
  unlinkSync(lockFile);
} catch {
  /* ignore */
}
console.log("[migrate] done");

── FILE: scripts/doctor.mjs  (259 خط) ──

/**
 * ابزار تشخیص و تعمیر دیتابیس (Doctor)
 * ------------------------------------------------
 *   node scripts/doctor.mjs                 بررسی سلامت و شمارش ردیف‌ها
 *   node scripts/doctor.mjs --restore FILE  بازیابی از یک فایل بک‌آپ (JSON)
 *   node scripts/doctor.mjs --repair        اگر دیتابیس باز نشد: قرنطینه + ساخت تازه + بازیابی از آخرین بک‌آپ
 *   node scripts/doctor.mjs --latest        بازیابی از آخرین فایل بک‌آپ
 *
 * نکته: سامانه باید خاموش باشد (قفل پوشهٔ داده بررسی می‌شود).
 */
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { existsSync, readFileSync, renameSync, unlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { mkdir, readdir, readFile, stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

const dataDir = process.env.PGLITE_DATA_DIR ?? ".data/pglite";
const backupDir = process.env.BACKUP_DIR ?? ".data/backups";
const lockFile = path.join(path.resolve(dataDir), ".pumpstation.lock");

/** جدول‌های بک‌آپ: کلید JSON → نام جدول در دیتابیس */
const TABLE_MAP = {
  valueAudits: "value_audits",
  processReadings: "process_readings",
  dailyTotals: "daily_totals",
  motorReadings: "motor_readings",
  motorStateLogs: "motor_state_logs",
  motorServices: "motor_services",
  meggerTests: "megger_tests",
  oilChanges: "oil_changes",
  equipmentReports: "equipment_reports",
  pmRecords: "pm_records",
  pmTemplates: "pm_templates",
  catalogs: "catalogs",
  alerts: "alerts",
  activity: "activity",
  problems: "problems",
  sendQueue: "send_queue",
  reports: "reports",
  operatorNotes: "operator_notes",
  motors: "motors",
  equipment: "equipment",
  channels: "channels",
  shifts: "shifts",
  settings: "settings",
};

const SERIAL_TABLES = [
  "value_audits", "process_readings", "daily_totals", "motor_readings", "motor_state_logs",
  "motor_services", "megger_tests", "oil_changes", "equipment_reports", "pm_records",
  "pm_templates", "catalogs", "alerts", "activity", "problems", "send_queue", "reports",
  "operator_notes", "equipment",
];

const snake = (key) => key.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (c) => `_${c.toLowerCase()}`);

function assertServerStopped() {
  if (!existsSync(lockFile)) return;
  try {
    const info = JSON.parse(readFileSync(lockFile, "utf8"));
    if (info?.pid) {
      try {
        process.kill(info.pid, 0);
        console.error(`سامانه در حال اجراست (PID ${info.pid}). ابتدا آن را ببندید.`);
        process.exit(1);
      } catch {
        console.warn(`قفل قدیمی از PID ${info.pid} باقی مانده؛ ادامه می‌دهیم.`);
      }
    }
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

async function openDb(dir) {
  const db = new PGlite(dir);
  await db.query("select 1");
  return db;
}

async function counts(db) {
  const out = {};
  for (const table of Object.values(TABLE_MAP)) {
    try {
      const r = await db.query(`select count(*)::int as n from "${table}"`);
      out[table] = r.rows[0].n;
    } catch {
      out[table] = null;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

async function listBackups() {
  await mkdir(backupDir, { recursive: true });
  const files = (await readdir(backupDir)).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json"));
  const rows = [];
  for (const file of files) {
    const full = path.join(backupDir, file);
    const info = await stat(full);
    rows.push({ file, full, bytes: info.size, mtime: info.mtimeMs });
  }
  return rows.sort((a, b) => b.mtime - a.mtime);
}

async function applySnapshot(db, snapshot) {
  const report = {};
  for (const [key, table] of Object.entries(TABLE_MAP)) {
    const rows = snapshot[key];
    if (!Array.isArray(rows)) continue;
    await db.query(`delete from "${table}"`);
    if (!rows.length) {
      report[table] = 0;
      continue;
    }
    const columns = Object.keys(rows[0]).map((c) => snake(c));
    const placeholders = columns.map((_, i) => `$${i + 1}`).join(",");
    const sql = `insert into "${table}" (${columns.map((c) => `"${c}"`).join(",")}) values (${placeholders})`;
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 200) {
      const chunk = rows.slice(i, i + 200);
      for (const row of chunk) {
        const values = Object.keys(rows[0]).map((c) => {
          const v = row[c];
          if (v === undefined) return null;
          if (v !== null && typeof v === "object") return JSON.stringify(v); // jsonb
          return v;
        });
        await db.query(sql, values);
      }
    }
    report[table] = rows.length;
  }
  for (const table of SERIAL_TABLES) {
    try {
      await db.query(`select setval(pg_get_serial_sequence('${table}','id'), greatest(coalesce((select max(id) from "${table}"),0),1))`);
    } catch {
      /* جدول بدون سری */
    }
  }
  return report;
}

async function runMigrations(db) {
  const files = (await readdir("drizzle")).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".sql")).sort();
  await db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "_migrations" ("name" text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL, "applied_at" text NOT NULL);`);
  const applied = new Set((await db.query(`SELECT name FROM "_migrations"`)).rows.map((r) => r.name));
  for (const file of files) {
    const name = file.replace(/\.sql$/, "");
    if (applied.has(name) || (name.startsWith("0000_") && applied.has("0000_baseline"))) continue;
    const raw = await readFile(path.join("drizzle", file), "utf8");
    const statements = raw.split("--> statement-breakpoint").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    for (const statement of statements) await db.exec(statement);
    await db.query(`INSERT INTO "_migrations" ("name","applied_at") VALUES ($1,$2)`, [name, new Date().toISOString()]);
    console.log(`[doctor] applied ${file}`);
  }
}

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const has = (flag) => args.includes(flag);
const valueOf = (flag) => {
  const i = args.indexOf(flag);
  return i >= 0 ? args[i + 1] : null;
};

assertServerStopped();

/* --------------------------------------------------- بررسی سلامت */
let healthy = true;
try {
  const db = await openDb(dataDir);
  const c = await counts(db);
  const missing = Object.entries(c).filter(([, n]) => n === null).map(([t]) => t);
  console.log("[doctor] دیتابیس باز شد.");
  if (missing.length) {
    console.log(`[doctor] جدول‌های ناموجود: ${missing.join(", ")}`);
    healthy = false;
  }
  const important = ["motor_readings", "process_readings", "equipment", "shifts", "settings", "activity"];
  for (const t of important) console.log(`  ${t}: ${c[t] ?? "—"}`);
  await db.close();
} catch (error) {
  healthy = false;
  console.log(`[doctor] دیتابیس خراب یا غیرقابل‌باز شدن است: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);
}

if (healthy && !has("--restore") && !has("--latest") && !has("--repair")) {
  const backups = await listBackups();
  console.log(`[doctor] بک‌آپ‌های موجود: ${backups.length}`);
  if (backups[0]) console.log(`  آخرین: ${backups[0].file} — ${(backups[0].bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`);
  console.log("[doctor] همه‌چیز مرتب است.");
  process.exit(0);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------- تعمیر/بازیابی */
let target = valueOf("--restore");
const backups = await listBackups();

if (!target && (has("--repair") || has("--latest"))) {
  if (!backups.length) {
    console.error("[doctor] هیچ فایل بک‌آپی پیدا نشد؛ بازیابی ممکن نیست.");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  target = backups[0].file;
}

if (target && !target.endsWith(".json")) target = `${target}.json`;
const full = path.resolve(backupDir, path.basename(target ?? ""));
if (!target || !existsSync(full)) {
  console.error(`[doctor] فایل بک‌آپ پیدا نشد: ${target}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const json = await readFile(full, "utf8");
const sha = existsSync(`${full}.sha256`) ? (await readFile(`${full}.sha256`, "utf8")).trim() : "";
const actual = createHash("sha256").update(json).digest("hex");
if (sha && sha !== actual) {
  console.error("[doctor] چک‌سام فایل با محتوا نمی‌خواند؛ برای جلوگیری از بازیابی مخدوش متوقف شد.");
  console.error("  اگر مطمئنید فایل سالم است، دستی بررسی کنید یا از فایل دیگری استفاده کنید.");
  process.exit(1);
}
const snapshot = JSON.parse(json);

let workingDir = dataDir;
if (!healthy) {
  const quarantine = `${dataDir}.broken-${new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-")}`;
  renameSync(path.resolve(dataDir), quarantine);
  console.log(`[doctor] نسخهٔ خراب به ${quarantine} منتقل شد.`);
  await mkdir(dataDir, { recursive: true });
  workingDir = dataDir;
}

const db = await openDb(workingDir);
writeFileSync(lockFile, JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, startedAt: Date.now() }), "utf8");
try {
  if (!healthy) await runMigrations(db);
  const report = await applySnapshot(db, snapshot);
  const total = Object.values(report).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  console.log(`[doctor] بازیابی انجام شد: ${total} ردیف از ${path.basename(full)}`);
  for (const [t, n] of Object.entries(report)) if (n) console.log(`  ${t}: ${n}`);
  const after = await counts(db);
  const bad = Object.entries(report).filter(([t, n]) => after[t] !== n);
  if (bad.length) {
    console.error("[doctor] شمارش پس از بازیابی تطبیق ندارد:", bad.map(([t, n]) => `${t} (${after[t]}/${n})`).join(", "));
    process.exitCode = 1;
  } else {
    console.log("[doctor] تطبیق شمارش ردیف‌ها تأیید شد.");
  }
} catch (error) {
  console.error("[doctor] بازیابی ناموفق:", error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
} finally {
  await db.close();
  try {
    unlinkSync(lockFile);
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

── FILE: scripts/reset-admin-pin.mjs  (83 خط) ──

/**
 * بازنشانی اضطراری PIN (وقتی کسی به سامانه دسترسی ندارد) — نسخهٔ ۱٫۴٫۱.
 *
 *   node scripts/reset-admin-pin.mjs --pin 123456 --confirm 123456 [--shift admin]
 *
 * بدون --pin و --confirmِ یکسان، هیچ کاری انجام نمی‌شود (PIN پیش‌فرضی در کار نیست).
 * شرط‌ها: سامانه باید خاموش باشد (قفل پوشهٔ داده بررسی می‌شود) و
 * رویداد در دفتر فعالیت ثبت می‌شود تا رد پا بماند.
 */
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { randomBytes, scryptSync } from "node:crypto";
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const flag = (name) => {
  const i = args.findIndex((a) => a === name);
  return i >= 0 ? (args[i + 1] ?? "").trim() : "";
};

const pin = flag("--pin");
const confirm = flag("--confirm");
const shiftKey = flag("--shift") || "admin";

if (!pin || !confirm) {
  console.error("استفاده: node scripts/reset-admin-pin.mjs --pin <4تا6 رقم> --confirm <تکرار PIN> [--shift admin]");
  console.error("بدون --pin و --confirm هیچ کاری انجام نمی‌شود.");
  process.exit(1);
}
if (pin !== confirm) {
  console.error("PIN و تکرار آن یکسان نیست؛ هیچ کاری انجام نشد.");
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!/^\d{4,6}$/.test(pin)) {
  console.error("PIN باید چهار تا شش رقم باشد.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const dataDir = process.env.PGLITE_DATA_DIR ?? ".data/pglite";
const lockFile = path.join(path.resolve(dataDir), ".pumpstation.lock");

if (existsSync(lockFile)) {
  try {
    const info = JSON.parse(readFileSync(lockFile, "utf8"));
    if (info?.pid) {
      try {
        process.kill(info.pid, 0);
        console.error(`سامانه در حال اجراست (PID ${info.pid}). ابتدا آن را ببندید.`);
        process.exit(1);
      } catch {
        /* قفل قدیمی */
      }
    }
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

const salt = randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
const hash = `scrypt$${salt}$${scryptSync(pin, salt, 32).toString("hex")}`;

const db = new PGlite(dataDir);
try {
  const exists = await db.query(`select count(*)::int as n from shifts where key = $1`, [shiftKey]);
  if (!exists.rows[0].n) {
    console.error(`شیفت «${shiftKey}» در دیتابیس نیست. کلیدهای موجود:`);
    const keys = await db.query(`select key from shifts order by key`);
    for (const row of keys.rows) console.error(`  - ${row.key}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  await db.query(`update shifts set pin = $1 where key = $2`, [hash, shiftKey]);
  await db.query(
    `insert into activity (at, shift_key, action, detail, prev_hash, hash) values ($1,$2,$3,$4,'','')`,
    [new Date().toLocaleString("fa-IR"), "system", "بازنشانی اضطراری PIN", `شیفت ${shiftKey} با اسکریپت محلی`],
  );
  console.log(`PIN شیفت «${shiftKey}» بازنشانی شد. پس از ورود، PIN را از تب ادمین عوض کنید.`);
} catch (error) {
  console.error("بازنشانی ناموفق:", error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
} finally {
  await db.close();
}

── FILE: scripts/mirror-backup.mjs  (74 خط) ──

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * کپی روزانهٔ بک‌آپ روی آینه (حافظهٔ بیرونی) — برای Windows Task Scheduler / cron
 *
 * روش کار: چون دیتابیس PGlite تک‌نویسنده است، اسکریپت خودش فایل دیتابیس را باز نمی‌کند؛
 * به سرور در حال اجرا یک درخواست محلی می‌فرستد و سرور نسخهٔ تازه می‌سازد، آن را روی
 * MIRROR_DIR کپی می‌کند و با sha256 مبدأ/مقصد تأیید می‌کند.
 *
 * استفاده:
 *   node scripts/mirror-backup.mjs
 *   BASE=http://127.0.0.1:3000 node scripts/mirror-backup.mjs
 *
 * خروجی: 0 = موفق، 1 = ناموفق (برای Task Scheduler تا هشدار بدهد)
 */
import { appendFile, mkdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const BASE = (process.env.BASE ?? "http://127.0.0.1:3000").replace(/\/$/, "");
const TOKEN_FILE = path.resolve(ROOT, process.env.MIRROR_TOKEN_FILE ?? ".data/mirror-token");
const LOG_FILE = path.resolve(ROOT, process.env.MIRROR_LOG ?? ".data/logs/mirror.log");

function stamp() {
  return new Date().toLocaleString("fa-IR", { timeZone: "Asia/Tehran" });
}

async function log(line) {
  try {
    await mkdir(path.dirname(LOG_FILE), { recursive: true });
    await appendFile(LOG_FILE, `${new Date().toISOString()}  ${line}\n`, "utf8");
  } catch {
    /* ثبت لاگ نباید اجرای اصلی را متوقف کند */
  }
}

async function main() {
  let token = "";
  try {
    token = (await readFile(TOKEN_FILE, "utf8")).trim();
  } catch {
    console.error(`✖ فایل توکن پیدا نشد: ${TOKEN_FILE}`);
    console.error("  یک‌بار از پنل ادمین «آینهٔ بک‌آپ» را باز کنید تا توکن ساخته شود، یا متغیر MIRROR_TOKEN_FILE را تنظیم کنید.");
    await log("FAIL: missing token file");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  let response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/mirror`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "x-mirror-token": token },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`✖ اتصال بهسرور برقرار نشد (${BASE}): ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);
    console.error("  سرور ایستگاه باید در حال اجرا باشد (سرویس ویندوز).");
    await log(`FAIL: connect ${BASE} — ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
    console.error(`✖ کپی ناموفق: ${data.error ?? response.status}`);
    await log(`FAIL: ${data.error ?? response.status}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  console.log(`✔ ${stamp()} — نسخهٔ ${data.file} (${(data.bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB) روی آینه کپی شد`);
  console.log(`  مسیر آینه: ${data.target}`);
  if (data.sha256) console.log(`  sha256: ${data.sha256.slice(0, 16)}…`);
  await log(`OK: ${data.file} ${data.bytes}B`);
}

await main();

── FILE: scripts/pg-init.mjs  (65 خط) ──

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * ساخت اسکیمای سامانه روی سرور PostgreSQL (به‌جای PGlite).
 * همهٔ فایل‌های drizzle/*.sql به ترتیب نام اجرا می‌شوند و در جدول `_migrations`
 * ثبت می‌شوند؛ بنابراین اجرای دوباره بی‌خطر است.
 *
 *   DATABASE_URL=postgresql://psuser:SECRET@127.0.0.1:5432/pumpstation node scripts/pg-init.mjs
 */
import { readFile, readdir } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import pg from "pg";

const url = (process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "").trim();
if (!/^postgres(ql)?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
  console.error("✖ DATABASE_URL تنظیم نشده است. مثال:");
  console.error("  DATABASE_URL=postgresql://psuser:SECRET@127.0.0.1:5432/pumpstation node scripts/pg-init.mjs");
  process.exit(1);
}

const dir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "drizzle");
const pool = new pg.Pool({ connectionString: url, max: 2 });

try {
  const who = await pool.query("select current_database() as db, current_user as usr, version() as v");
  console.log(`→ اتصال به دیتابیس «${who.rows[0].db}» با کاربر «${who.rows[0].usr}»`);
  console.log(`  ${String(who.rows[0].v).split(",")[0]}`);

  await pool.query("create table if not exists _migrations (name text primary key, applied_at timestamptz not null default now())");
  const applied = new Set((await pool.query("select name from _migrations")).rows.map((r) => r.name));

  const files = (await readdir(dir)).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".sql")).sort();
  for (const file of files) {
    if (applied.has(file)) {
      console.log(`[migrate] skip ${file}`);
      continue;
    }
    const sql = await readFile(path.join(dir, file), "utf8");
    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
      await client.query("begin");
      await client.query(sql);
      await client.query("insert into _migrations (name) values ($1)", [file]);
      await client.query("commit");
      console.log(`[migrate] applied ${file}`);
    } catch (error) {
      await client.query("rollback").catch(() => {});
      console.error(`✖ خطا در ${file}: ${error.message}`);
      process.exitCode = 1;
      break;
    } finally {
      client.release();
    }
  }

  const tables = await pool.query(
    "select count(*)::int as n from information_schema.tables where table_schema = 'public'",
  );
  console.log(`[migrate] done — ${tables.rows[0].n} جدول در public`);
} catch (error) {
  console.error(`✖ اتصال/مهاجرت ناموفق: ${error.message}`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
} finally {
  await pool.end();
}

── FILE: scripts/pg-dump.mjs  (73 خط) ──

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * پشتیبان‌گیری روزانه از PostgreSQL با pg_dump (فرمت custom، فشرده).
 * مناسب Windows Task Scheduler / cron. نسخه‌های قدیمی خودکار پاک می‌شوند.
 *
 *   DATABASE_URL=postgresql://... node scripts/pg-dump.mjs
 *
 * متغیرها:
 *   PGDUMP_DIR   پوشهٔ مقصد        (پیش‌فرض: .data/pgdump)
 *   PGDUMP_KEEP  تعداد نسخهٔ نگه‌داری (پیش‌فرض: 14)
 *   PGDUMP_BIN   مسیر pg_dump.exe  (اگر در PATH نیست)
 *   MIRROR_DIR   اگر ست باشد، نسخهٔ تازه همان‌جا هم کپی می‌شود
 */
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { copyFile, mkdir, readdir, stat, unlink } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";

const url = (process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "").trim();
if (!/^postgres(ql)?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
  console.error("✖ DATABASE_URL تنظیم نشده است.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const outDir = path.resolve(process.cwd(), process.env.PGDUMP_DIR ?? ".data/pgdump");
const keep = Number(process.env.PGDUMP_KEEP ?? 14);
const bin = process.env.PGDUMP_BIN?.trim() || "pg_dump";
const mirrorDir = process.env.MIRROR_DIR?.trim() ? path.resolve(process.env.MIRROR_DIR) : "";

const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, "").replace("T", "-").slice(0, 15);
const file = path.join(outDir, `pumpstation-${stamp}.dump`);

function run(command, args) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(command, args, { stdio: ["ignore", "inherit", "pipe"], env: process.env });
    let stderr = "";
    child.stderr.on("data", (d) => {
      stderr += String(d);
    });
    child.on("error", (error) => reject(new Error(`اجرای ${command} ممکن نشد: ${error.message}`)));
    child.on("close", (code) => (code === 0 ? resolve() : reject(new Error(`${command} با کد ${code} خارج شد — ${stderr.trim()}`))));
  });
}

async function main() {
  await mkdir(outDir, { recursive: true });
  console.log(`→ pg_dump به ${file}`);
  await run(bin, ["--format=custom", "--compress=9", "--no-owner", "--no-privileges", `--file=${file}`, url]);

  const info = await stat(file);
  if (info.size < 1024) throw new Error("فایل دامپ غیرعادی کوچک است؛ عملیات ناموفق تلقی می‌شود");
  console.log(`✔ حجم دامپ: ${(info.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`);

  if (mirrorDir) {
    await mkdir(mirrorDir, { recursive: true });
    await copyFile(file, path.join(mirrorDir, path.basename(file)));
    console.log(`✔ کپی روی آینه: ${mirrorDir}`);
  }

  const files = (await readdir(outDir)).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".dump")).sort();
  for (const old of files.slice(0, Math.max(0, files.length - keep))) {
    await unlink(path.join(outDir, old)).catch(() => {});
    console.log(`  حذف نسخهٔ قدیمی: ${old}`);
  }
  console.log(`✔ نگه‌داری ${keep} نسخهٔ آخر در ${outDir}`);
}

try {
  await main();
} catch (error) {
  console.error(`✖ ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

── FILE: scripts/pg-import-json.mjs  (205 خط) ──

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * انتقال دادهٔ موجود (فایل بک‌آپ JSON نسخهٔ PGlite) به سرور PostgreSQL.
 *
 *   DATABASE_URL=postgresql://psuser:SECRET@127.0.0.1:5432/pumpstation \
 *     node scripts/pg-import-json.mjs --file .data/backups/pumpstation-1405-08-13_20-51-55.json
 *
 *   بدون --file: آخرین فایل بک‌آپ در .data/backups (یا MIRROR_DIR) انتخاب می‌شود.
 *
 * ویژگی‌ها:
 *  • پیش از نوشتن، هش فایل با کنارِ آن (‎.sha256‎) بررسی می‌شود.
 *  • همه‌چیز در یک تراکنش است؛ هر خطا = هیچ تغییری در مقصد.
 *  • نگاشت camelCase→snake_case انجام و با information_schema اعتبارسنجی می‌شود.
 *  • پیش‌فرض: مقصد باید خالی باشد (برای جلوگیری از دوباره‌نویسی)؛ با --replace پاک می‌شود.
 */
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { readFile, readdir, stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import pg from "pg";

const url = (process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "").trim();
if (!/^postgres(ql)?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
  console.error("✖ DATABASE_URL تنظیم نشده است.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const fileArg = args.includes("--file") ? args[args.indexOf("--file") + 1] : "";
const replace = args.includes("--replace");

/** کلید شیء در فایل بک‌آپ → نام جدول در PostgreSQL (هم‌ترتیب با TABLES در src/lib/backup.ts) */
const TABLE_MAP = {
  shifts: "shifts",
  settings: "settings",
  motors: "motors",
  equipment: "equipment",
  channels: "channels",
  catalogs: "catalogs",
  pmTemplates: "pm_templates",
  pmRecords: "pm_records",
  equipmentReports: "equipment_reports",
  motorReadings: "motor_readings",
  motorStateLogs: "motor_state_logs",
  processReadings: "process_readings",
  dailyTotals: "daily_totals",
  valueAudits: "value_audits",
  motorServices: "motor_services",
  meggerTests: "megger_tests",
  oilChanges: "oil_changes",
  alerts: "alerts",
  activity: "activity",
  problems: "problems",
  sendQueue: "send_queue",
  reports: "reports",
  operatorNotes: "operator_notes",
};

const snake = (s) => s.replace(/[A-Z]/g, (m) => `_${m.toLowerCase()}`);

async function pickFile() {
  if (fileArg) return path.resolve(fileArg);
  const dirs = [".data/backups", process.env.MIRROR_DIR ?? "", ".data/mirror"].filter(Boolean);
  for (const dir of dirs) {
    try {
      const files = (await readdir(path.resolve(dir))).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json")).sort().reverse();
      if (files.length) return path.resolve(dir, files[0]);
    } catch {
      /* پوشه نیست */
    }
  }
  throw new Error("هیچ فایل بک‌آپی پیدا نشد؛ مسیر را با --file بدهید");
}

async function verifyHash(file) {
  try {
    const expected = (await readFile(`${file}.sha256`, "utf8")).trim();
    const actual = createHash("sha256").update(await readFile(file)).digest("hex");
    if (expected && expected !== actual) throw new Error("هش فایل با فایل .sha256 یکسان نیست؛ فایل آسیب دیده است");
    return expected ? "مهر تأیید هش درست است" : "فایل .sha256 ندارد";
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ENOENT") return "فایل .sha256 ندارد";
    throw error;
  }
}

async function main() {
  const file = await pickFile();
  const info = await stat(file);
  console.log(`→ فایل بک‌آپ: ${file} (${(info.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)`);
  console.log(`  ${await verifyHash(file)}`);

  const snapshot = JSON.parse(await readFile(file, "utf8"));
  const pool = new pg.Pool({ connectionString: url, max: 2 });
  const client = await pool.connect();

  try {
    await client.query("begin");

    // اگر مقصد داده دارد و --replace نبوده، متوقف شو
    const existing = await client.query("select count(*)::int as n from shifts");
    if (existing.rows[0].n > 0 && !replace) {
      throw new Error("دیتابیس مقصد داده دارد. برای بازنویسی کامل --replace بدهید یا مقصد تازه بسازید");
    }

    // ترتیب درج مهم نیست اگر تریگرهای کلید خارجی موقتاً خاموش شوند
    let replicationRole = false;
    try {
      await client.query("set session_replication_role = replica");
      replicationRole = true;
    } catch {
      const tables = Object.values(TABLE_MAP);
      for (const t of tables) await client.query(`alter table if exists ${t} disable trigger all`).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (replace) {
      for (const t of Object.values(TABLE_MAP)) await client.query(`delete from ${t}`).catch(() => {});
    }

    const columnsCache = new Map();
    let total = 0;
    for (const [key, table] of Object.entries(TABLE_MAP)) {
      const rows = snapshot[key];
      if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) continue;

      if (!columnsCache.has(table)) {
        const res = await client.query(
          "select column_name from information_schema.columns where table_schema='public' and table_name=$1",
          [table],
        );
        columnsCache.set(table, new Set(res.rows.map((r) => r.column_name)));
      }
      const cols = columnsCache.get(table);
      if (cols.size === 0) {
        console.warn(`  ! جدول ${table} در مقصد نیست؛ ${rows.length} ردیف رد شد`);
        continue;
      }

      const first = rows[0];
      const mapped = Object.keys(first).map((k) => [k, cols.has(snake(k)) ? snake(k) : null]);
      const use = mapped.filter(([, c]) => c);
      const dropped = mapped.filter(([, c]) => !c).map(([k]) => k);
      if (dropped.length) console.warn(`  ! ستون‌های ناشناخته در ${table}: ${dropped.join(", ")}`);

      const chunk = 200;
      for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += chunk) {
        const slice = rows.slice(i, i + chunk);
        const values = [];
        const tuples = slice.map((row, r) => {
          const placeholders = use.map(([k], c) => {
            let v = row[k];
            if (v !== null && typeof v === "object") v = JSON.stringify(v);
            values.push(v === undefined ? null : v);
            return `$${r * use.length + c + 1}`;
          });
          return `(${placeholders.join(",")})`;
        });
        const sql = `insert into ${table} (${use.map(([, c]) => c).join(",")}) values ${tuples.join(",")}`;
        await client.query(sql, values);
      }
      total += rows.length;
      console.log(`  ✔ ${table}: ${rows.length} ردیف`);
    }

    // هم‌تراز کردن شمارنده‌های سری
    const serials = await client.query(`
      select c.relname as table_name, a.attname as column_name
      from pg_class c
      join pg_attribute a on a.attrelid = c.oid
      join pg_depend d on d.objid = a.attrelid and d.refobjid = c.oid
      where c.relkind = 'r' and a.attname = 'id' and a.attnum > 0
        and pg_get_serial_sequence(c.relname, 'id') is not null
    `);
    for (const row of serials.rows) {
      await client
        .query(
          `select setval(pg_get_serial_sequence('${row.table_name}','id'),
                         coalesce((select max(id) from ${row.table_name}), 1))`,
        )
        .catch(() => {});
    }

    if (replicationRole) await client.query("set session_replication_role = default");
    else {
      for (const t of Object.values(TABLE_MAP)) await client.query(`alter table if exists ${t} enable trigger all`).catch(() => {});
    }

    await client.query("commit");
    console.log(`✔ انتقال کامل شد — ${total} ردیف در ${Object.keys(snapshot).length} گروه`);
    console.log("  قدم بعد: سرور را با همان DATABASE_URL بالا بیاورید و در پنل ادمین یک بک‌آپ تازه بگیرید.");
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback").catch(() => {});
    throw error;
  } finally {
    client.release();
    await pool.end();
  }
}

try {
  await main();
} catch (error) {
  console.error(`✖ ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

── FILE: scripts/serve.mjs  (70 خط) ──

/**
 * اجرای سرور با بایند امن (نسخهٔ ۱٫۴٫۱).
 *
 *   node scripts/serve.mjs dev [--webpack] [--host lan|IP]
 *   node scripts/serve.mjs start [--host lan|IP]
 *
 * پیش‌فرض فقط 127.0.0.1 است. حالت شبکهٔ داخلی فقط با BIND=lan یا ‎--host
 * صریح فعال می‌شود (همراه با اخطار فایروال). جزئیات: docs/STATION_NETWORK_FA.md
 */
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { resolveBind } from "../src/lib/netbind.ts";
import { requireLocalSqlite } from "../src/lib/sqlite-cloud.ts";

// پیش‌بررسی مسیر دیتابیس (حالت PGlite): مسیر ابری = توقف پیش از بالا آمدن
if (!/^postgres(ql)?:\/\//i.test((process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? "").trim())) {
  try {
    requireLocalSqlite(process.env.PGLITE_DATA_DIR ?? ".data/pglite");
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

const [mode, ...rest] = process.argv.slice(2);
if (mode !== "dev" && mode !== "start") {
  console.error("استفاده: node scripts/serve.mjs dev|start [--webpack] [--host lan|IP]");
  process.exit(1);
}

let hostOverride = "";
const passthrough = [];
for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i += 1) {
  if (rest[i] === "--host" && rest[i + 1]) {
    hostOverride = rest[i + 1];
    i += 1;
    continue;
  }
  passthrough.push(rest[i]);
}

let host;
try {
  host = (hostOverride ? resolveBind(hostOverride) : resolveBind()).host;
} catch (error) {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
  process.exit(1);
}

const port = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);
if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
  console.error(`پورت نامعتبر است: ${process.env.PORT}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

if (host === "0.0.0.0") {
  console.warn(`[net] هشدار: سرور روی همهٔ رابط‌های شبکه باز می‌شود (${host}:${port}) — فقط در شبکهٔ داخلی ایستگاه با فایروال محدود به رنج اداره.`);
} else {
  console.log(`[net] server on ${host}:${port} (loopback-only)`);
}

const child = spawn(
  process.execPath,
  ["node_modules/next/dist/bin/next", mode, "-H", host, "-p", String(port), ...passthrough],
  { stdio: "inherit", cwd: process.cwd() },
);
child.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
  if (signal) process.exit(1);
  process.exit(code ?? 0);
});

── FILE: scripts/bulk-import.mjs  (206 خط) ──

/**
 * ورود گروهی قرائت‌ها از CSV از مسیر رسمی API (نسخهٔ ۱٫۴٫۱).
 *
 *   node scripts/bulk-import.mjs --file rows.csv --shift A --pin 1011 --confirm-test
 *   node scripts/bulk-import.mjs --file rows.csv --dry-run   (بدون نوشتن؛ فقط بررسی و گزارش)
 *
 * ستون‌های CSV (با سطر سربرگ): day,interval,scope,tag,param,value
 *   scope=process → tag مثل PIT-001 (param نادیده) — یک درخواست برای هر سطر
 *   scope=motor   → tag مثل M-01 و param مثل I1 — سطرهای هم‌روز/هم‌بازه/هم‌موتور یکی می‌شوند
 *   scope=total   → فقط value (یک عدد برای هر روز)
 *
 * ایمنی: بدون --confirm-test هیچ چیزی نوشته نمی‌شود (--dry-run استثناست)؛
 * پیش‌فرض فقط به خودِ دستگاه وصل می‌شود (--allow-remote برای آدرس بیرونی).
 * هر درخواست کلید یکتایی قطعی دارد؛ اگر وسط کار قطع شود، اجرای دوباره
 * سطرهای قبلی را تکرارِ بی‌اثر می‌کند (نه ثبت دوباره).
 */
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { isValidJalaliDay } from "../src/lib/jalali.ts";

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const flag = (name) => {
  const i = args.findIndex((a) => a === name);
  return i >= 0 ? (args[i + 1] ?? "").trim() : "";
};
const has = (name) => args.includes(name);

const file = flag("--file");
const shift = flag("--shift") || "A";
const pin = flag("--pin");
const base = (flag("--base") || "http://127.0.0.1:3000").replace(/\/$/, "");
const chunkSize = Math.max(1, Number(flag("--chunk") || 50));
const dryRun = has("--dry-run");
const confirmed = has("--confirm-test");

if (!file) {
  console.error("استفاده: node scripts/bulk-import.mjs --file rows.csv --shift A --pin **** [--dry-run | --confirm-test]");
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!dryRun && !confirmed) {
  console.error("بدون --confirm-test هیچ چیزی نوشته نمی‌شود (تأیید می‌کنید دیتابیس مقصد آزمایشی است). برای بررسی بدون نوشتن: --dry-run");
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!dryRun && !pin) {
  console.error("--pin لازم است.");
  process.exit(1);
}
let baseUrl;
try {
  baseUrl = new URL(base);
} catch {
  console.error(`آدرس مقصد نامعتبر است: ${base}`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const localHost = ["127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1"].includes(baseUrl.hostname);
if (!localHost && !has("--allow-remote")) {
  console.error(`مقصد ${base} دستگاه خودمان نیست؛ برای ادامه --allow-remote را اضافه کنید.`);
  process.exit(1);
}

function parseCsv(text) {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l && !l.startsWith("#"));
  if (lines.length < 2) return { header: [], rows: [] };
  const split = (line) => line.split(",").map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ""));
  const header = split(lines[0]).map((h) => h.toLowerCase());
  return { header, rows: lines.slice(1).map((line, i) => ({ lineNo: i + 2, cells: split(line) })) };
}

const { header, rows } = parseCsv(readFileSync(file, "utf8"));
const need = ["day", "interval", "scope", "tag", "param", "value"];
const missing = need.filter((c) => !header.includes(c));
if (missing.length) {
  console.error(`ستون‌های لازم در CSV نیست: ${missing.join("، ")} (لازم: ${need.join(",")})`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const col = (row, name) => row.cells[header.indexOf(name)] ?? "";

const failures = [];   // { lineNo, reason }
const requests = [];   // { action, payload }
const motorGroups = new Map(); // day|interval|tag -> { day, intervalIndex, tag, values }

for (const row of rows) {
  const fail = (reason) => failures.push({ lineNo: row.lineNo, reason });
  const day = col(row, "day");
  const intervalIndex = Number(col(row, "interval"));
  const scope = col(row, "scope").toLowerCase();
  const tag = col(row, "tag");
  const param = col(row, "param");
  const value = Number(col(row, "value"));
  if (!isValidJalaliDay(day)) { fail(`روز نامعتبر: ${day}`); continue; }
  if (!Number.isInteger(intervalIndex) || intervalIndex < 0 || intervalIndex > 5) { fail(`بازه نامعتبر: ${col(row, "interval")}`); continue; }
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) { fail(`مقدار نامعتبر: ${col(row, "value")}`); continue; }
  if (scope === "process") {
    if (!tag) { fail("تگ خالی است"); continue; }
    requests.push({ action: "process.set", payload: { tag, value, day, intervalIndex } });
  } else if (scope === "motor") {
    if (!tag || !param) { fail("تگ یا پارامتر خالی است"); continue; }
    const key = `${day}|${intervalIndex}|${tag}`;
    if (!motorGroups.has(key)) motorGroups.set(key, { day, intervalIndex, tag, values: {} });
    motorGroups.get(key).values[param] = value;
  } else if (scope === "total") {
    requests.push({ action: "total.set", payload: { value, day } });
  } else {
    fail(`نوع نامعتبر (process/motor/total): ${col(row, "scope")}`);
  }
}
for (const g of motorGroups.values()) {
  requests.push({ action: "motor.values", payload: { tag: g.tag, values: g.values, day: g.day, intervalIndex: g.intervalIndex } });
}

console.log(`سطرها: ${rows.length} | درخواست‌ها: ${requests.length} | سطر خراب: ${failures.length}`);
if (dryRun) {
  console.log("(حالت بررسی: چیزی نوشته نشد.)");
  for (const f of failures.slice(0, 20)) console.log(`  سطر ${f.lineNo}: ${f.reason}`);
  if (failures.length > 20) console.log(`  … و ${failures.length - 20} مورد دیگر`);
  process.exit(failures.length ? 1 : 0);
}

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const idemKey = (req) => `bulk-${createHash("sha1").update(JSON.stringify(req)).digest("hex").slice(0, 32)}`;

const login = await fetch(`${base}/api/auth`, {
  method: "POST",
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({ key: shift, pin }),
});
if (!login.ok) {
  console.error(`ورود ناموفق (${login.status})؛ شیفت/PIN یا آدرس مقصد را بررسی کنید.`);
  process.exit(1);
}
const cookie = (login.headers.get("set-cookie") || "").split(";")[0];
if (!cookie) {
  console.error("ورود کوکی برنگرداند؛ ادامه ممکن نیست.");
  process.exit(1);
}

let ok = 0, replayed = 0;
const sendFailures = [];
for (let i = 0; i < requests.length; i += 1) {
  const req = requests[i];
  let attempt = 0;
  for (;;) {
    attempt += 1;
    const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/actions`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie, "Idempotency-Key": idemKey(req) },
      body: JSON.stringify(req),
    });
    if (res.status === 429 && attempt <= 4) {
      const wait = Math.min(300, Math.max(1, Number(res.headers.get("Retry-After") ?? 60)));
      console.log(`  … سقف نرخ؛ ${wait} ثانیه صبر و ادامه (درخواست ${i + 1}/${requests.length})`);
      await sleep(wait * 1000);
      continue;
    }
    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    if (res.ok && body?.ok) {
      if (res.headers.get("idempotent-replay")) replayed += 1; else ok += 1;
    } else {
      sendFailures.push({ n: i + 1, action: req.action, error: body?.error ?? `HTTP ${res.status}` });
    }
    break;
  }
  if ((i + 1) % chunkSize === 0 && i + 1 < requests.length) {
    console.log(`  … ${i + 1}/${requests.length} ارسال شد؛ مکث کوتاه`);
    await sleep(1000);
  }
}

// نمونه‌بینی: یک قلم فرآیندی و یک قلم موتوری را از تاریخچه می‌خوانیم
const verify = [];
const sampleProcess = requests.find((r) => r.action === "process.set");
const sampleMotor = requests.find((r) => r.action === "motor.values");
async function checkHistory(url, needle) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { cookie } });
    const text = await res.text();
    return res.ok && text.includes(needle);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
if (sampleProcess) {
  const p = sampleProcess.payload;
  const good = await checkHistory(
    `${base}/api/history?scope=process&tag=${encodeURIComponent(p.tag)}&from=${encodeURIComponent(p.day)}&to=${encodeURIComponent(p.day)}`,
    String(p.value),
  );
  verify.push(`نمونهٔ فرآیندی ${p.tag}=${p.value}: ${good ? "پیدا شد ✓" : "پیدا نشد ✗"}`);
}
if (sampleMotor) {
  const p = sampleMotor.payload;
  const firstParam = Object.keys(p.values)[0];
  const good = await checkHistory(
    `${base}/api/history?scope=motor&tag=${encodeURIComponent(p.tag)}&param=${encodeURIComponent(firstParam)}&from=${encodeURIComponent(p.day)}&to=${encodeURIComponent(p.day)}`,
    String(p.values[firstParam]),
  );
  verify.push(`نمونهٔ موتوری ${p.tag}/${firstParam}=${p.values[firstParam]}: ${good ? "پیدا شد ✓" : "پیدا نشد ✗"}`);
}

console.log("--- گزارش ---");
console.log(`موفق: ${ok} | تکراریِ بی‌اثر: ${replayed} | ناموفق: ${sendFailures.length} | سطر خراب: ${failures.length}`);
for (const f of failures.slice(0, 10)) console.log(`  سطر ${f.lineNo}: ${f.reason}`);
for (const f of sendFailures.slice(0, 10)) console.log(`  درخواست ${f.n} (${f.action}): ${f.error}`);
for (const v of verify) console.log(`  ${v}`);
process.exit(sendFailures.length || failures.length ? 1 : 0);

── FILE: audit/audit1.mjs  (161 خط) ──

const BASE = (process.env.BASE ?? "http://127.0.0.1:3000").replace(/\/$/, "");
const out = [];
function log(name, pass, detail) {
  out.push({ name, pass, detail });
  console.log(`${pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"} | ${name} | ${detail}`);
}
async function login(key, pin) {
  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/auth`, {
    method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ key, pin }),
  });
  const cookie = (r.headers.get("set-cookie") || "").split(";")[0];
  return { status: r.status, cookie, body: await r.json().catch(() => null) };
}
async function act(cookie, action, payload) {
  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, {
    method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie },
    body: JSON.stringify({ action, payload }),
  });
  return { status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => null) };
}
const H = (cookie) => ({ cookie });

(async () => {
  // ---------- A: authentication ----------
  const bad = await login("A", "0000");
  log("wrong PIN rejected", bad.status === 401, `status=${bad.status}`);

  // ---- per-IP lock after 5 wrong PINs ----
  const attacker = { "x-forwarded-for": "10.9.9.9" };
  const post = (body, headers = {}) => fetch(`${BASE}/api/auth`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...headers }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
  let firstLock = 0;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    const r = await post({ key: "B", pin: String(1000 + i) }, attacker);
    if (r.status === 429 && !firstLock) firstLock = i + 1;
  }
  const perAttempt = (Date.now() - t0) / 6;
  log("brute-force throttling (per IP)", firstLock > 0 && firstLock <= 5, `قفل از تلاش ${firstLock} ام فعال شد (${perAttempt.toFixed(1)}ms/attempt) — ادامهٔ حدس بی‌فایده است`);

  // legitimate user from another IP is not affected by the attacker's lock
  const legitOtherIp = await post({ key: "A", pin: "1011" }, { "x-forwarded-for": "10.1.1.1" });
  log("other IP not locked out by attacker", legitOtherIp.status === 200, `status=${legitOtherIp.status}`);

  // global shift lock after 20 failures from rotating IPs
  let globalLock = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
    const r = await post({ key: "C", pin: String(2000 + i) }, { "x-forwarded-for": `10.8.8.${i}` });
    if (r.status === 429 && !globalLock) globalLock = i + 1;
  }
  log("global shift lock (IP rotation)", globalLock > 0 && globalLock <= 25, `شیفت C پس از ${globalLock} تلاش قفل شد`);

  // پاک‌سازی قفل‌های ایجادشده در تست‌های حدس PIN تا مجموعهٔ بعدی سالم بماند
  {
    const adm = await login("admin", "9009");
    if (adm.cookie) {
      await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie: adm.cookie }, body: JSON.stringify({ action: "admin.unlock", payload: { key: "all" } }) });
    }
  }

  const okA = await login("A", "1011");
  log("valid PIN accepted", okA.status === 200 && okA.cookie.includes("ps_session"), `status=${okA.status}`);
  const A = okA.cookie;

  const admin = await login("admin", "9009");
  // تست‌های قبلی ممکن است قفل ورود گذاشته باشند؛ ادمین آن را برمی‌دارد
  await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie: admin.cookie }, body: JSON.stringify({ action: "admin.unlock", payload: { key: "all" } }) });
  const ADM = admin.cookie;
  log("admin login", admin.status === 200, `status=${admin.status}, role=${admin.body?.user?.role}`);

  // ---------- B: session forgery with the default secret ----------
  const { createHmac } = await import("node:crypto");
  const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ key: "admin", at: Date.now() })).toString("base64url");
  const sig = createHmac("sha256", "pumpstation-local-preview-secret").update(payload).digest("base64url");
  const forged = `ps_session=${payload}.${sig}`;
  const forgedRes = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state`, { headers: { cookie: forged } });
  const forgedBody = await forgedRes.json().catch(() => null);
  log("forged cookie rejected", forgedRes.status === 401,
    `status=${forgedRes.status}${forgedRes.status === 200 ? ` — forged admin session WORKS (me=${forgedBody?.me?.key}/${forgedBody?.me?.role})` : ""}`);

  // malformed / tampered cookie
  const tampered = A.replace(/=([^.]*)\./, "=x");
  const tamperedRes = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state`, { headers: { cookie: tampered } });
  log("tampered cookie rejected", tamperedRes.status === 401, `status=${tamperedRes.status}`);

  // ---------- C: unauthenticated access ----------
  const targets = [
    ["/api/state", "GET"], ["/api/totals", "GET"], ["/api/history?param=PIT-001", "GET"],
    ["/api/catalogs?id=1", "GET"], ["/api/backups/1", "GET"], ["/report/1", "GET"], ["/totals", "GET"],
  ];
  for (const [path, method] of targets) {
    const r = await fetch(BASE + path, { method, redirect: "manual" });
    const blocked = [401, 403, 307, 302].includes(r.status);
    log(`anon ${path}`, blocked, `status=${r.status}`);
  }
  const anonAct = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, {
    method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ action: "total.set", payload: { value: 1 } }),
  });
  log("anon POST /api/actions", anonAct.status === 401, `status=${anonAct.status}`);

  // ---------- D: authorization ----------
  const operatorAdmin = await act(A, "admin.pin", { key: "admin", pin: "1234" });
  log("operator cannot change PIN", operatorAdmin.status === 400 && /ادمین/.test(operatorAdmin.body?.error ?? ""), `status=${operatorAdmin.status} error=${operatorAdmin.body?.error}`);
  const operatorBackup = await act(A, "backup.create", {});
  log("operator cannot create backup", operatorBackup.status === 400, `status=${operatorBackup.status} error=${operatorBackup.body?.error}`);
  const admState = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state`, { headers: H(ADM) }).then((r) => r.json());
  log("admin sees admin panel data", admState.me.role === "admin", `role=${admState.me.role}`);

  // ---------- E: input validation / injection ----------
  const sqli = await act(A, "process.set", { tag: "PIT-001' OR 1=1--", value: 12, day: "1405/08/13", intervalIndex: 0 });
  log("SQLi in tag rejected", sqli.status === 400, `status=${sqli.status} error=${sqli.body?.error}`);
  const sqli2 = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/history?scope=process&tag=${encodeURIComponent("PIT-001' OR '1'='1")}&param=x&days=5`, { headers: H(A) });
  const sqli2Body = await sqli2.json().catch(() => null);
  log("SQLi in query string handled", sqli2.status < 500, `status=${sqli2.status} body=${JSON.stringify(sqli2Body).slice(0, 120)}`);
  const huge = await act(A, "process.set", { tag: "PIT-001", value: 999999999999, day: "1405/08/13", intervalIndex: 0 });
  log("absurd value rejected", huge.status === 400, `status=${huge.status} error=${huge.body?.error}`);
  const negative = await act(A, "total.set", { value: -5000, day: "1405/08/13" });
  log("negative total rejected", negative.status === 400, `status=${negative.status} ${negative.body?.ok ? "ACCEPTED (bug)" : "error=" + negative.body?.error}`);
  // قفل خوش‌بینانه: متن فعلی را می‌خوانیم تا ثبت با تعارض رد نشود

  const xss = await act(A, "note.save", { text: '<script>alert(1)</script><img src=x onerror=alert(1)>', day: noteDay, expected: cur.note ?? "" });
  const stateAfter = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state`, { headers: H(A) }).then((r) => r.json());
  log("XSS payload stored as inert text", stateAfter.note.includes("<script>") && xss.body?.ok, `stored raw, rendered by React escaping. len=${stateAfter.note.length}`);

  log("over-long note truncated silently", st2.note.length <= 4000, `saved length=${st2.note.length} (silently truncated, no warning)`);

  const badDay = await act(A, "process.set", { tag: "PIT-001", value: 5, day: "1405/13/45", intervalIndex: 0 });
  log("invalid jalali date accepted?", badDay.status === 400, `status=${badDay.status} ${badDay.body?.ok ? "ACCEPTED invalid date 1405/13/45 (bug)" : "error=" + badDay.body?.error}`);

  // ---------- G: races ----------
  const day = "1405/08/13";
  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => act(A, "process.set", { tag: "PIT-002", value: 5 + i, day, intervalIndex: 1 })));

  log("concurrent same-process writes (8x)", true, `value shown=${st.process.find((p) => p.tag === "PIT-002")?.value}`);

  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => act(A, "motor.values", { tag: "EM-01", day, intervalIndex: 1, values: { I1: 100 + i } })));
  const dupCheck = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/history?scope=motor&tag=EM-01&param=I1&from=${day}&to=${day}`, { headers: H(A) }).then((r) => r.json());
  const pts = dupCheck.points ?? [];
  const sameDay = pts.filter((p) => String(p.label ?? "").includes("11") || true).length;
  log("concurrent motor value writes duplicate rows?", pts.length <= 6, `points=${pts.length} for 6 intervals — ${pts.length > 6 ? "DUPLICATES (bug)" : "ok"}`);

  // ---------- H: megger (multi-voltage + per-winding PI + unit) ----------
  const mg = await act(A, "motor.megger", { tag: "M-01", day, testVoltage: 1000, testVoltageRotor: 500, unit: "GΩ", statorU: 120, statorV: 130, statorW: 125, rotorU: 90, rotorV: 95, rotorW: 92, windingTemp: 35, piStatorU: 2.1, piStatorV: 2.0, piStatorW: 2.2, piRotor: 1.8, note: "audit" });
  log("megger multi-voltage/PI/unit accepted", mg.status === 200 && mg.body?.ok === true && typeof mg.body?.id === "number", `status=${mg.status} id=${mg.body?.id}`);
  st = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state?day=${day}&interval=1`, { headers: H(A) }).then((r) => r.json());
  const mrow = (st.meggers ?? []).find((m) => m.tag === "M-01");
  log("megger fields persisted", mrow?.testVoltageRotor === 500 && mrow?.unit === "GΩ" && mrow?.piStatorU === 2.1 && mrow?.piRotor === 1.8, `rotorV=${mrow?.testVoltageRotor} unit=${mrow?.unit} piU=${mrow?.piStatorU} piR=${mrow?.piRotor}`);
  const mgBad = await act(A, "motor.megger", { tag: "M-01", day, testVoltage: 1000, testVoltageRotor: 9999, statorU: 1 });
  log("megger invalid rotor voltage rejected", mgBad.status === 400, `status=${mgBad.status}`);
  const mgLegacy = await act(A, "motor.megger", { tag: "M-02", day, testVoltage: 2500, statorU: 50, pi: 1.5 });
  log("megger legacy payload (single PI) still works", mgLegacy.status === 200 && mgLegacy.body?.ok === true, `status=${mgLegacy.status}`);

  console.log("\n=== summary ===");
  console.log(`total=${out.length} pass=${out.filter((o) => o.pass).length} fail=${out.filter((o) => !o.pass).length}`);
  console.log(JSON.stringify(out.filter((o) => !o.pass).map((o) => o.name), null, 1));
})();

const BASE = (process.env.BASE ?? "http://127.0.0.1:3000").replace(/\/$/, "");
const out = [];

async function login(key, pin) {
  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/auth`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ key, pin }) });
  return (r.headers.get("set-cookie") || "").split(";")[0];
}

  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie }, body: JSON.stringify({ action, payload }) });
  return { status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => null) };

(async () => {
  const A = await login("A", "1011");

  // ---- 6 parallel entries for a brand-new day total (unique(day) constraint) ----

  log("parallel total.set on empty day", totals.every((t) => t.body?.ok), JSON.stringify(totals.map((t) => t.body?.error ?? "ok")));

  // ---- backup / restore round-trip ----
  const before = await state(A);
  const bk = await act(ADM, "backup.create", {});
  log("create backup", bk.body?.ok === true, `id=${bk.body?.id}`);
  // add data after backup
  const extra = await act(A, "total.set", { value: 4242, day: "1405/09/02" });
  const restore = await act(ADM, "backup.restore", { id: bk.body?.id });
  const after = await state(A);
  log("restore executes", restore.body?.ok === true, `status=${restore.status} err=${restore.body?.error ?? "-"}`);
  log("restore rolls data back", after.counts.equipment === before.counts.equipment, `equipment ${before.counts.equipment}->${after.counts.equipment}, readings ${before.counts.readings}->${after.counts.readings}`);
  // now insert new rows after restore — sequence collision?
  const post1 = await act(A, "problem.add", { text: "تست بعد از بازیابی یک" });
  const post2 = await act(A, "problem.add", { text: "تست بعد از بازیابی دو" });
  log("insert after restore", post1.body?.ok && post2.body?.ok, `first=${post1.body?.error ?? "ok"} second=${post2.body?.error ?? "ok"}`);
  const post3 = await act(A, "report.create", { kind: "daily", day });
  log("create report after restore", post3.body?.ok === true, `err=${post3.body?.error ?? "ok"} id=${post3.body?.id}`);

  // ---- report authorization (object level) ----

  const B = await login("B", "2022");
  const repB = await fetch(`${BASE}/report/${post3.body?.id ?? 1}`, { headers: { cookie: B } });
  log("other shift can read report by id", repB.status === 200, `status=${repB.status} — reports are not scoped per shift (by design?)`);

  const fd = new FormData();

  fd.append("file", new Blob([Buffer.from("<html><script>alert(1)</script></html>")], { type: "application/pdf" }), "fake.pdf");

  const upBody = await up.json().catch(() => null);
  log("fake PDF (HTML content) rejected", up.status === 400, `status=${up.status} ${upBody?.ok ? "ACCEPTED — content not validated (bug)" : "err=" + upBody?.error}`);
  if (upBody?.ok) {
    const file = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/catalogs?id=${upBody.id}`, { headers: { cookie: A } });
    log("stored file served with forced content-type", true, `ct=${file.headers.get("content-type")} disp=${file.headers.get("content-disposition")} nosniff=${file.headers.get("x-content-type-options") ?? "MISSING"}`);
  }
  const fd2 = new FormData();
  fd2.append("tag", "EM-01");
  fd2.append("file", new Blob([Buffer.alloc(9 * 1024 * 1024)], { type: "application/pdf" }), "big.pdf");
  const up2 = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/catalogs`, { method: "POST", headers: { cookie: ADM }, body: fd2 });
  log("oversize upload rejected", [400, 413].includes(up2.status), `status=${up2.status} (413 = توسط سقف حجم بدنه رد شد)`);

  // ---- offline queue (simulated: channel unconfigured) ----
  const send = await act(A, "report.send", { kind: "daily" });
  log("send with unconfigured channel queues", send.body?.ok === true, `msg=${send.body?.message}`);

  const badInt = await act(A, "process.set", { tag: "PIT-001", value: 1, day, intervalIndex: 99 });
  log("interval 99 rejected", badInt.status === 400, `${badInt.body?.error}`);
  const badAct = await act(A, "system.nuke", {});
  log("unknown action rejected", badAct.status === 400, `${badAct.body?.error}`);
  const malformed = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie: A }, body: "{oops" });
  log("malformed JSON handled", malformed.status === 400, `status=${malformed.status}`);
  const noType = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, { method: "POST", headers: { cookie: A }, body: "action=total.set" });
  log("non-JSON content-type handled", noType.status === 400, `status=${noType.status}`);

  // ---- security headers ----
  const home = await fetch(`${BASE}/`, { headers: { cookie: A } });
  const hdrs = ["content-security-policy", "x-frame-options", "x-content-type-options", "strict-transport-security", "referrer-policy"];
  log("security headers present", hdrs.some((h) => home.headers.get(h)), hdrs.map((h) => `${h}=${home.headers.get(h) ?? "-"}`).join(", "));

  // ---- payload size / DoS ----
  const big = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, {
    method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie: A },
    body: JSON.stringify({ action: "note.save", payload: { text: "x".repeat(6 * 1024 * 1024) } }),
  });
  log("6MB body rejected", big.status !== 200, `status=${big.status} ${big.status === 200 ? "ACCEPTED (no body-size limit)" : ""}`);

  console.log(JSON.stringify(out.filter((o) => !o.pass).map((o) => o.name), null, 1));

/**
 * تست‌های نسخه ۱.۳ — تاب‌آوری، نشست سروری، ETag، شناسهٔ یکتا، CSRF، بایگانی و خروجی CSV
 * اجرا: node audit/audit3.mjs   (سرور باید روی 127.0.0.1:3000 بالا باشد)
 */
const BASE = process.env.BASE ?? "http://127.0.0.1:3000";
let pass = 0;
const fails = [];
function log(name, ok, extra = "") {
  if (ok) pass += 1;
  else fails.push(`${name} ${extra}`);
  console.log(`${ok ? "PASS" : "FAIL"} | ${name}${extra ? ` | ${extra}` : ""}`);
}

async function login(key, pin) {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/auth`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ key, pin }),
  });
  const cookie = (res.headers.get("set-cookie") ?? "").split(";")[0];
  return { status: res.status, cookie, body: await res.json().catch(() => ({})) };
}

async function act(cookie, action, payload = {}, headers = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie, ...headers },
    body: JSON.stringify({ action, payload }),
  });
  return { status: res.status, body: await res.json().catch(() => ({})), headers: res.headers };
}

  log("health has no secrets", !JSON.stringify(body).includes("secret"));
}

{

  log("session.list", Array.isArray(list.body.sessions) && list.body.sessions.length > 0, `count=${list.body.sessions?.length}`);
  // یک نشست اختصاصی برای شیفت C می‌سازیم تا ابطال تک‌نشست را دقیق بسنجیم

  const target = fresh.body.sessions.find((s) => s.id.startsWith(me.sessionId ?? "-"));

    log("revoked session rejected", probe.status === 401, `status=${probe.status}`);
  } else {
    log("session.revoke", false, "نشست هدف پیدا نشد");
  }

  const after = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state`, { headers: { cookie: third.cookie } }).then((r) => r.status);

{
  const before = await act(admin.cookie, "data.stats", {});

    // ردیف‌های بایگانی‌شده باید در نمودار چندساله دیده شوند (نمای ترکیبی)
    const trend = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/history?scope=motor&tag=M-01&param=T1&days=3650`, { headers: { cookie: admin.cookie } }).then((r) => r.json());
    log("archived rows still visible in charts", Array.isArray(trend.points) && trend.points.length > 0, `points=${trend.points?.length}`);
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------- ۸) دفتر فعالیت و خطاها */
{
  const chain = await act(admin.cookie, "audit.verify", { limit: 5000 });
  log("activity hash chain valid", chain.body.ok === true && chain.body.brokenAtId === null, `checked=${chain.body.checked}`);
  const events = await act(admin.cookie, "events.list", {});
  log("events.list", Array.isArray(events.body.events));
}

/* ------------------------------------------- ۹) سطل بازیافت تجهیزات */
{
  const tag = `TST-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`;
  const created = await act(admin.cookie, "equipment.save", { tag, name: "تجهیز آزمایشی", category: "پمپ‌ها" });
  const list = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/state`, { headers: { cookie: admin.cookie } }).then((r) => r.json());
  const row = list.equipment.find((e) => e.tag === tag);
  log("equipment created", created.body.ok === true && Boolean(row));
  if (row) {
    await act(admin.cookie, "equipment.delete", { id: row.id, reason: "تست" });
    const trash = await act(admin.cookie, "equipment.trash", {});
    const inTrash = trash.body.items?.some((x) => x.id === row.id);
    log("equipment moved to trash", Boolean(inTrash));
    const restored = await act(admin.cookie, "equipment.restore", { id: row.id });
    log("equipment restored", restored.body.ok === true);
    await act(admin.cookie, "equipment.delete", { id: row.id, reason: "پاک‌سازی تست" });
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------- ۱۰) نگه‌داری: بهینه‌سازی و بک‌آپ */
{
  const files = await act(admin.cookie, "backup.files", {});
  log("backup.files", Array.isArray(files.body.files));
  if (files.body.files?.length) {
    const verify = await act(admin.cookie, "backup.verify", { file: files.body.files[0].file });
    log("backup.verify", verify.body.ok === true && verify.body.checksumOk === true, `counts=${Object.keys(verify.body.counts ?? {}).length}`);
  }
  const optimize = await act(admin.cookie, "data.optimize", {});
  log("data.optimize", optimize.body.ok === true, optimize.body.message ?? "");
}

/* ------------------------------------------------- ۱۱) موارد منفی */
{
  const unknown = await act(admin.cookie, "not.an.action", {});
  log("unknown action 400", unknown.status === 400, `status=${unknown.status}`);
  const huge = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/actions`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", cookie: admin.cookie },
    body: JSON.stringify({ action: "note.save", payload: { text: "x".repeat(400_000) } }),
  });
  log("oversize body rejected", [400, 413].includes(huge.status), `status=${huge.status}`);
  const badDay = await act(admin.cookie, "process.set", { tag: "PIT-001", value: 1, day: "1405/13/45" });
  log("invalid jalali day rejected", badDay.status === 400, `status=${badDay.status}`);
}

console.log(`\n=== summary ===\ntotal=${pass + fails.length} pass=${pass} fail=${fails.length}`);
if (fails.length) console.log(fails);
process.exit(fails.length ? 1 : 0);
